- **supply-knowledge-retrieve**: Retrieves the ERP/MRP supply position, supplier master, BOM/where-used and open orders relevant to a disruption and attaches them plus the supplier's delivery history to the mfg.supplier-disruption.v1 contract. Use when the user says "what does this part go into", "where is this used", "what's our on-hand and lead time", "is this single-source", "has this supplier been late before", or after disruption-intake completes. ([TedTschopp/cat-agent-skills](https://github.com/TedTschopp/cat-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/submissions/supplier-disruption/skills/supply-knowledge-retrieve))
- **supply-lane-tuning**: Tune the BBS Supply channel (Grainger MRO resale; internal track "supply", pricing vehicle NASPO MRO LPA) of the BBS quote bot — system prompt, product surfacing, sourcing/pricing guardrails, voice, and cost. Use whenever the ask is about the Supply lane's chat behavior — "it shows too many products", "limit the options", "recommend one", "the Grainger chat isn't smooth", pricing/facility rules, "make it cheaper to run", or aligning the model with the channel's goals. Covers where the tuning levers live, the grounding rules, and how to verify with the scripted eval. ([Black-Box-Safety/bbs-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/Black-Box-Safety/bbs-claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/bbs-website/skills/supply-lane-tuning))
- **supply-logistics-simulation**: Simulates logistics routing scenarios for lead time and cost trade-offs. ([nahisaho/coreclaw-marketplace](https://github.com/nahisaho/coreclaw-marketplace/tree/HEAD/coreclaw-skills-hub/skills/supply/supply-logistics-simulation))
